Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SS13 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG3
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Barkod Üretimden Malzeme Ýadesi
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set Doc3 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK83", "")

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA2=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA2=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "HEDEF_BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARGETAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPS_ICIN_TRANSFER","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "STOK25_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "STOK83_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK=STACK","IO")
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "REZ_IPTAL","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "HEDEF_BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "HEDEF_BARKOD_AV"
	
	'Dim Par
	'Set Par=Nothing
	'Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK2512_M",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK25T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")	

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, Len(LocPrefix)) = LocPrefix Then
		Call AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Else
		Call StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	End if
End Sub
Sub HEDEF_BARKOD_AV()
	Call StokBilgileriniDoldur2()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	YeniL1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
	YeniL2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
	YeniL3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
	YeniL4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = YeniL1
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = YeniL2
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = YeniL3
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = YeniL4

	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", "KOD='" & D7LB.SKOD & "'&SERINO='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
'	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD

	if S01.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		MTable.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE
		STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE

		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1
		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2
		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3
		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4
		STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
		STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID = S01.STOK03_ID

		MTable.JOBNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	End if
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if trim(MTable.STOK83_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK83", "")
		Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK83_EVRAKNO
		if Doc1.Delete_Voucher <> 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.STOK83_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu MPS Ýçin Transfer Fiþi silinemedi", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		Else
			Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		End if
	End if

	if trim(MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýptal iþlemi baþarýyla gerçekleþti.", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
	Else
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
End Sub
Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA2) Then Set STOK01_PDA2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA2")
	Set MSTCK = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK")

	MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = ""	
	MTable.STOK83_EVRAKNO = ""	

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR >= STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI Then
		Set S01List = Doc.Select2("sdfw3fsdf", "STOK01_PDA", "", "", -1)
		S01List.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
		Kalan = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR - STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
		if Kalan > 0 Then
			Set S01List2 = Doc.Select2("sdfw3fsdfa", "STOK01_PDA", "", "", -1)
			S01List2.SF_MIKTAR = -1 * Kalan
			S01List.AppendTable(S01List2)
		End if
	Else
		Set S01List = GetS01List()
	End if

	if S01List.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Call STOK25EvrakYarat(S01List)
		TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
		MTable.Empty
		STOK01_PDA.Empty
		STOK01_PDA2.Empty
		MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = TREvrakNo
	End if
	if MSTCK.REZ_IPTAL = "E" and trim(MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		Call MPSIcinTransferFisiniOlustur()
	End if
End Sub
Function GetS01List()
	Set GetS01List = Doc.Select1("cdre3dfw23f", "STOK01", "KOD='xx____ddde3LL'", "", -1)

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	KalanMiktar = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	if trim(STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID) <> "" Then
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "STOK01", "STOK03_ID='" & STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID & "'", "", -1)

		GetS01List.AppendTable(S01)
		KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - S01.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
	JobNo = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	if trim(JobNo) = "" Then JobNo = GetTransferJobNo()
	QStr = "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	if trim(JobNo) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_MPSARTNO='" & JobNo & "'"
		if trim(STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & "&STOK03_ID<>'" & STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID & "'"
		End if
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34ffdfg", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)

		For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
			if S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) < KalanMiktar Then
				EklenenMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Else
				EklenenMiktar = KalanMiktar
			End if

			S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) = EklenenMiktar

			KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - EklenenMiktar
		Next
		Set S01_1 = Doc.Select2("sf23fsdfsdf4f4", S01.GetAppName, "SF_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
		GetS01List.AppendTable(S01_1)
	End if

	if KalanMiktar > 0 Then
		QStr = "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'&ALLOC_MPSARTNO='" & Space(16) & "'"
		if trim(STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & "&STOK03_ID<>'" & STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID & "'"
		End if
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34ffdfg", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)
		For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
			if S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) < KalanMiktar Then
				EklenenMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Else
				EklenenMiktar = KalanMiktar
			End if

			S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) = EklenenMiktar

			KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - EklenenMiktar
		Next
		Set S01_1 = Doc.Select2("sf23fsdfsdf4f4", S01.GetAppName, "SF_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
		GetS01List.AppendTable(S01_1)
	End if
	if KalanMiktar > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.AMBCODE) & " deposunda bu malzemeden istenilen miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		GetS01List.Empty
	End if
End Function
Function GetTransferJobNo()
	GetTransferJobNo = ""
	Set S10A = Doc.Select1("wdfr4r4rfdf4t", "STOK10A", "SERINO='" & STOK01_PDA.SERINO & "'&EVRAKTYPE='U2'", "", -1)
	if S10A.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetTransferJobNo = S10A.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
End Function
Sub STOK25EvrakYarat(S01List)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA2) Then Set STOK01_PDA2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA2")
	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S25E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTable.TARGETAMBCODE


	For i = 1 to S01List.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S25T.AddRow()
		S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		S25T.KOD = S01List.KOD(i)
		S25T.LOTNUMBER = S01List.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S25T.SERINO = S01List.SERINO(i)
		S25T.SF_MIKTAR = Abs(S01List.SF_MIKTAR(i))
		S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT = S01List.SF_UNIT(i)
		S25T.FOKOD = S01List.FOKOD(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C = S01List.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01List.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01List.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01List.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1 = S01List.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2 = S01List.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION1 = S01List.LOCATION1(i)
		S25T.LOCATION2 = S01List.LOCATION2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION3 = S01List.LOCATION3(i)
		S25T.LOCATION4 = S01List.LOCATION4(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01List.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01List.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01List.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01List.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01List.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01List.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01List.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01List.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01List.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)

		Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA"
		if trim(STOK01_PDA2.KOD) = "" or trim(STOK01_PDA2.SERINO) = "" Then
			if S01List.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
				S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
				S25T.NEWSERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
				S25T.NEWFOKOD = STOK01_PDA.FOKOD
				S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C
				S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1
				S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2
				S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3
				S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1
				S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2
				S25T.NEWLOCATION1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
				S25T.NEWLOCATION2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
				S25T.NEWLOCATION3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
				S25T.NEWLOCATION4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
			Else
				S25T.NEWSERINO = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S01List.AMBCODE(i)
			End if
		Else
			S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA2.LOTNUMBER
			S25T.NEWSERINO = STOK01_PDA2.SERINO
			S25T.NEWFOKOD = STOK01_PDA2.FOKOD
			S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C = STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_C
			S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_R1
			S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_R2
			S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_R3
			S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1 = STOK01_PDA2.VRI_NUM1
			S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2 = STOK01_PDA2.VRI_NUM2
			S25T.NEWLOCATION1 = STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION1
			S25T.NEWLOCATION2 = STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION2
			S25T.NEWLOCATION3 = STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION3
			S25T.NEWLOCATION4 = STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION4
			S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = STOK01_PDA2.AMBCODE
		End if
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then 
		MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Call YeniEtiketKartlariniYarat(S25T)
	End if
	'Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub YeniEtiketKartlariniYarat(S25T)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(i)), 30), "")) = "" Then
			if not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
				DocYaratildi = True
				Set D7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
			End if
			if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.SERINO(i)), 30), "")) <> "" Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T.SERINO(i)
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
				D7LB.SERINO = S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				D7LB.ONCEKETIKET = S25T.SERINO(i)
			Else
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				d7LB.SKOD = S25T.NEWCODE(i)
				d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK25"
				d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
				d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i)
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_UNIT = S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
				d7LB.FOKOD = S25T.NEWFOKOD(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_C = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)		
			End if
			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub MPSIcinTransferFisiniOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	AllocStr = "ALLOC_SYSSTATUS+ALLOC_NONMOVABL+ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE+ALLOC_EVRAKNO+ALLOC_SIPTYPE+ALLOC_SIPNO+ALLOC_SIPARTNO+ALLOC_MPSEVNO+ALLOC_MPSARTNO"

	Set S25T = Doc.Select1G("sdf34fdsfds", "STOK25T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO & "'&ALLOC_MPSARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "'", "", "NEWCODE+NEWSERINO+" & AllocStr,-1)

	if S25T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set S25E = Doc.Select1("asefd32dfsdf", "STOK25E", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK83", "")
	Set S83E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set S83T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S83E.AMBCODE = S25E.TARGETAMBCODE
	S83E.TARGETAMBCODE = S25E.TARGETAMBCODE
	S83E.MPSGIRISCIKISTYPE = "-"
	
	S83T.Empty
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S83T.AddRow()
		S83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		S83T.JOBNO = S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
		S83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "H"
		S83T.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR
		S83T.SF_SF_UNIT = S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT

		S83T.KOD = S25T.NEWCODE
		S83T.LOTNUMBER = S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER
		S83T.SERINO = S25T.NEWSERINO
		S83T.FOKOD = S25T.NEWFOKOD
		S83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C
		S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1
		S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2
		S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3
		S83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1
		S83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2
		S83T.LOCATION1 = S25T.NEWLOCATION1
		S83T.LOCATION2 = S25T.NEWLOCATION2
		S83T.LOCATION3 = S25T.NEWLOCATION3
		S83T.LOCATION4 = S25T.NEWLOCATION4
		S83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = S25T.NEWLOCATION1
		S83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = S25T.NEWLOCATION2
		S83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = S25T.NEWLOCATION3
		S83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = S25T.NEWLOCATION4
		S83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
		S83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
		S83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
		S83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = S25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
		S83T.OALLOC_SIPNO = S25T.ALLOC_SIPNO
		S83T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = S25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
		S83T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = S25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
		S83T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	Next

	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if nret = 0 Then MTable.STOK83_EVRAKNO = S83E.EVRAKNO

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur2()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA2")
	Barkod = MTable.HEDEF_BARKOD
	MTable.HEDEF_BARKOD = ""

	STOK01_PDA2.Empty

	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", "KOD='" & D7LB.SKOD & "'&SERINO='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() <> 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Hedef barkod stoklarda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	STOK01_PDA2.KOD= S01.KOD
	STOK01_PDA2.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA2.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA2.SERINO = S01.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA2.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA2.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA2.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA2.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA2.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD

	STOK01_PDA2.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE

	STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1
	STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2
	STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3
	STOK01_PDA2.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4
	STOK01_PDA2.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	STOK01_PDA2.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	STOK01_PDA2.STOK03_ID = S01.STOK03_ID
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SS13 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG4
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Etiket Birleþtirme
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARGETAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPS_ICIN_TRANSFER","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "STOK25_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"
	'Dim Par
	'Set Par=Nothing
	'Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK2512_M",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if trim(STACK_PDA.PROPT) = "1" Then
		Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
		if MT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu Barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
			Exit Sub
		End if

		if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if D7LB.SKOD <> MTableT.KOD(1) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan Etiketin Stok Kodu öncekinden farklý olamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
				MTable.BARKOD = ""
				STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
				Exit Sub
			End if
		End if
		Call StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Else
		Call Cikar()
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()	
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		YeniAlloc = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

		AskiAlloc = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(1)

		if YeniAlloc <> EskiAlloc Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Rezervasyon bilgisi farklý olan etiketler birleþtirilemez", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub	
		End if
	End if


	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE", _
					"ALLOC_SYSSTATUS", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", "ALLOC_SIPTYPE", "ALLOC_SIPNO", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", _
					"ALLOC_MPSEVNO", "ALLOC_MPSARTNO", "STOK03_ID")

	For each Alan in S01Arr
		AlanDegeri = STOK01_PDA.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
		MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
	Next
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Cikar()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)

	Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
	Else
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if MTableT.SERINO(i) = D7LB.SERINO Then
				MTableT.KOD(i) = ""
				MTableT.SF_UNIT(i) = ""
				MTableT.SafePack()
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

'	YeniL1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
'	YeniL2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
'	YeniL3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
'	YeniL4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = YeniL1
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = YeniL2
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = YeniL3
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = YeniL4

	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", "KOD='" & D7LB.SKOD & "'&SERINO='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan barkoda stok kaydý bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if S01.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan barkoda ait birden çok stok satýrý bulundu", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD

	MTable.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE

	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID = S01.STOK03_ID

	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = S01.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	if trim(MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Kod/Lot/Seri Transfer Fiþi iptal edildi", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
	End if
End Sub
Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()

	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S25E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)

	S25T.Empty

	Set S01List = MTableT
	For i = 1 to S01List.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S25T.AddRow()
		S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		S25T.KOD = S01List.KOD(i)
		S25T.LOTNUMBER = S01List.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S25T.SERINO = S01List.SERINO(i)
		S25T.SF_MIKTAR = S01List.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT = S01List.SF_UNIT(i)
		S25T.FOKOD = S01List.FOKOD(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C = S01List.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01List.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01List.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01List.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1 = S01List.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2 = S01List.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION1 = S01List.LOCATION1(i)
		S25T.LOCATION2 = S01List.LOCATION2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION3 = S01List.LOCATION3(i)
		S25T.LOCATION4 = S01List.LOCATION4(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01List.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01List.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01List.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01List.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01List.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01List.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01List.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01List.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01List.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		S25T.AMBCODE_TR = S01List.AMBCODE(i)
	Next

	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"

	Set S25T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdfg34gfg5t5", "STOK25T", "", "<SF_MIKTAR", 1)
	YeniSeriNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.SetCurrentRow(i)
		S25T.NEWCODE = S25T1.KOD(1)
		S25T.NEWSERINO = YeniSeriNo
		S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER = S25T1.LOTNUMBER(1)
		S25T.NEWFOKOD = S25T1.FOKOD(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_C(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_R1(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_R2(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_R3(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_NUM1(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_NUM2(1)
		S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S25T1.AMBCODE_TR(1)
		S25T.NEWLOCATION1 = ""
		S25T.NEWLOCATION2 = ""
		S25T.NEWLOCATION3 = ""
		S25T.NEWLOCATION4 = ""
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then 
		MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Call YeniEtiketKartlariniYarat(Doc1, S25T)
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
	MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = TREvrakNo
End Sub
Sub YeniEtiketKartlariniYarat(Doc2, S25T1)
	Set S25T = Doc2.Select2G("sdf34rg5ggr", S25T1.GetAppName, "", "", "NEWSERINO", -1)
	Set S25T2 = Doc2.Select2("sdf34rg5gg__r", S25T1.GetAppName, "", "<SF_MIKTAR", 1)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(i)), 30), "")) = "" Then
			if not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
				DocYaratildi = True
				Set D7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
			End if
			if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T1.SERINO(i)), 30), "")) <> "" Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T1.SERINO(i)
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
				D7LB.SERINO = S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				D7LB.ONCEKETIKET = S25T2.SERINO(1)
				D7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				D7LB.AMBCODE1 = S25T2.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
			Else
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				d7LB.SKOD = S25T.NEWCODE(i)
				d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK25"
				d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
				d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i)
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_UNIT = S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
				d7LB.FOKOD = S25T.NEWFOKOD(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_C = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)		
				D7LB.AMBCODE1 = S25T2.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
			End if
			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if trim(TREvrakNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK25E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO, "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	if S25T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdgf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(1)),30) & "'", "", -1)
		if D7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			EtiketFormKodu = D7LB.FORMKODU
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu, "PrintLabel"
		End if
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SS13 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG5
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Nerede
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.AddField2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
	
	SET ST01=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	ST01.Addfield2 "STKKOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	ST01.Setd7FieldEvent "STKKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "STKKOD_AFV"

	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddField2 "LOKASYON","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
End Sub

Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Barkod = STOK01_PDA.BARKOD
	STOK01_PDA.BARKOD = ""
	if Left(Barkod, 4) = "%JB%" Then
		IsNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Mid(Barkod, 5)), 16)
		KaynakTuru = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE", IsNo, "JOBNO")
		if KaynakTuru = "H" Then
			StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKKODU", IsNo, "JOBNO")
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz barkod", "", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
	Else
		StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Barkod, "")
		if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
			BarkodMu = True
		End if
	End if
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then
		StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Barkod), 24), "")
	End if
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz barkod", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	STOK01_PDA.KOD = StokKodu
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
	
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfhd", "STOK01", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "AMBCODE+LOTNUMBER+LOCATION1+LOCATION2+LOCATION3", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 and Not BarkodMu Then
		STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
		STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(1)
	Elseif BarkodMu Then
		STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SF_BAKIYE", Barkod, "")
		STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SF_UNIT", Barkod, "")
		Set S011 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfhFFFd", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
		if S011.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = S011.AMBCODE
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = S011.LOCATION1
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = S011.LOCATION2
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = S011.LOCATION3
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = S011.LOCATION4
		End if
	End if

	Call Sirala(S01)

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirno)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOKASYON = trim(S01.LOCATION1(i)) & trim(S01.LOCATION2(i)) & trim(S01.LOCATION3(i)) & trim(S01.LOCATION4(i))
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	if STOK01_PDA_LIST.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
	End if
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub Sirala(S01)
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "MA" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "01"
		Elseif trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "MB" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "02"
		Elseif trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "GKO" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "03"
		Elseif trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "KALÝTE" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "04"
		Else
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "99"
		End if
	Next
	S01.Sort("VRI_VR_R1")

	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = ""
	Next
End Sub

SUB STKKOD_AFV
	SET ST01=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	SET S01= DOC.SELECT1("ASSDDUU","STOK01","KOD='"&ST01.STKKOD&"'&SERINO<>''","",-1)

	IF S01.GETRECCOUNT > 0 THEN
		ST01.D_ID2=S01.SERINO(1)
	ELSE
		MSGBOX "Bu koda ait SERÝ yoktur."
	END IF

	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK01_PDA","D_ID2",0 

END SUB

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SS13 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG5_X
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Nerede - BUL KUTUSU OLMAYAN - ESKÝ MAKRO - 11.03.2013
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.AddField2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddField2 "LOKASYON","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Barkod = STOK01_PDA.BARKOD
	STOK01_PDA.BARKOD = ""
	if Left(Barkod, 4) = "%JB%" Then
		IsNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Mid(Barkod, 5)), 16)
		KaynakTuru = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE", IsNo, "JOBNO")
		if KaynakTuru = "H" Then
			StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKKODU", IsNo, "JOBNO")
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz barkod", "", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
	Else
		StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Barkod, "")
		if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
			BarkodMu = True
		End if
	End if
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then
		StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Barkod), 24), "")
	End if
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz barkod", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	STOK01_PDA.KOD = StokKodu
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
	
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfhd", "STOK01", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "AMBCODE+LOTNUMBER+LOCATION1+LOCATION2+LOCATION3", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 and Not BarkodMu Then
		STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
		STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(1)
	Elseif BarkodMu Then
		STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SF_BAKIYE", Barkod, "")
		STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SF_UNIT", Barkod, "")
		Set S011 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfhFFFd", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
		if S011.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = S011.AMBCODE
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = S011.LOCATION1
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = S011.LOCATION2
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = S011.LOCATION3
			STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = S011.LOCATION4
		End if
	End if

	Call Sirala(S01)

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirno)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOKASYON = trim(S01.LOCATION1(i)) & trim(S01.LOCATION2(i)) & trim(S01.LOCATION3(i)) & trim(S01.LOCATION4(i))
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	if STOK01_PDA_LIST.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
	End if
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub Sirala(S01)
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "MA" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "01"
		Elseif trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "MB" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "02"
		Elseif trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "GKO" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "03"
		Elseif trim(S01.AMBCODE(i)) = "KALÝTE" Then
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "04"
		Else
			S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = "99"
		End if
	Next
	S01.Sort("VRI_VR_R1")

	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) = ""
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32T_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"

End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(D7LB.JOBNO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan Barkod operasyon barkodu deðil", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdfwgsdg", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & d7LB.JOBNO & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox d7LB.JOBNO & " no.lu iþ geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub		
	End if
	if trim(STACK_PDA.PROPT) = "1" Then
		Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
		if MT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu Barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if

		Set S32T = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "SFDC32T", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
		if S32T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu Barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if

		Call StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Else
		Call Cikar()
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()	
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Call CalcOpMiktar(MTable.MPSNO, MTable.JOBNO, BuOpToplamMiktar, OncekiOpMiktar)
	'msgbox BuOpToplamMiktar & ", " & OncekiOpMiktar
	if BuOpToplamMiktar > OncekiOpMiktar and OncekiOpMiktar <> -1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Önceki Operasyonun Tamamlanan Miktarý  :" & OncekiOpMiktar & vbcrlf & "Bu operasyonun Tamamlanan miktarý : " & BuOpToplamMiktar & vbcrlf & "giriþ yapýlamaz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	if HammaddeYok(MTable.JOBNO, STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI) Then Exit Sub

	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE", _
					"ALLOC_SYSSTATUS", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", "ALLOC_SIPTYPE", "ALLOC_SIPNO", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", _
					"ALLOC_MPSEVNO", "ALLOC_MPSARTNO", "STOK03_ID")

	For each Alan in S01Arr
		AlanDegeri = STOK01_PDA.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
		MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
	Next
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI

	MTableT.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
	MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
	MTableT.OPERASYON = MTable.OPERASYON
	MTableT.TEZGAH = MTable.TEZGAH
	MTableT.JOBNO = MTable.JOBNO

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Cikar()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
	Else
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if MTableT.SERINO(i) = D7LB.SERINO Then
				MTableT.KOD(i) = ""
				MTableT.SF_UNIT(i) = ""
				MTableT.SafePack()
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdfwgsdg", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & d7LB.JOBNO & "'", "", -1)
	
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD

	MTable.MPSNO = D7LB.MPSNO
	MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM = D7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM
	MTable.JOBNO = D7LB.JOBNO
	MTable.OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON
	MTable.OPERASYON_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10T.R_OPERASYON, "")
	MTable.TEZGAH = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU
	MTable.TEZGAH_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU , "")

	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = D7LB.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
'	MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO = SFDC32EvrakNo
'	MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM = SFDC32T_TRNum

	if trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) = "" or trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if UretimFisiniSil(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO, MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) <> 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GettableObject("SFDC32T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO
	if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		if trim(SFDC32T.TRNUM) = trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) Then
			if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Çalýþma Bildirimi iptal edildi", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildiriminde " & vbcrlf & _
								trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) & " no.lu satýr bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Ýptal", MB_OK
			MTable.Empty		
		End if
	Else
		Silindi = False
		For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(SFDC32T.TRNUM(i)) = trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) Then
				SFDC32T.DeleteRowNoUI i
				Silindi = True
			End if
		Next
		if Silindi Then
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Çalýþma Bildirimi kaydedildi.", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildiriminde " & vbcrlf & _
								trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) & " no.lu satýr bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Ýptal", MB_OK
			MTable.Empty		
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub CalismaBildirimiOlustur()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("sdsdfsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "", "JOBNO", -1)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.JOBNO(i) <> EskiIsNo Then
			Call CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)
			Doc1.New_Voucher()
			SFDC32E.MPSNO = MTableT.MPSNO(i)
			SFDC32E.JOBNO = MTableT.JOBNO(i)
		End if
		EskiIsNo = MTableT.JOBNO(i)

		if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 1 and SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR = 0 Then
			satirNo = 1
		Else
			satirNo = SFDC32T.AddRow()
		End if
		SFDC32T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		SFDC32T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SFDC32T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
		SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	Call CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)

	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT1.Empty
End Sub
Sub CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)
	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	if trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(SFDC32T.SERINO(i)) = "" Then
			SFDC32T.KOD(i) = ""
			SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i) = ""
			SFDC32T.SF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
			SFDC32T.RECETEKOD(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	SFDC32E.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_SURE_KAYITLARI"
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then 
		ret1 = SonOpIseUretimFisiKes(Doc1, SFDC32E.MPSNO, SFDC32E.JOBNO)
		if ret1 = 0 Then Call EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildirimi kaydedilemedi", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	exit sub
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if trim(TREvrakNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK25E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO, "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	if S25T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdgf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(1)),30) & "'", "", -1)
		if D7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			EtiketFormKodu = D7LB.FORMKODU
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu, "PrintLabel"
		End if
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function SonOpIseUretimFisiKes(Doc1, MPSNo, IsNo)
	SonOpIseUretimFisiKes = 0
	if Not SonOpMu(MPSNo, IsNo) Then Exit Function

	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	SFDC32T.SafePack()
	if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set STOK20E = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set STOK20T = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Doc2.New_Voucher
	STOK20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
	STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
	STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"
	
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = SFDC32E.MPSNO
		STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		STOK20T.KOD = SFDC32T.KOD(i)
		STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = SFDC32T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK20T.SERINO = SFDC32T.SERINO(i)
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK20T.W_FIRMA = Doc1.Getd7FirmCode()
		STOK20T.W_APP = Doc1.GetDocType()
		STOK20T.W_TABLE = "SFDC32T"
		STOK20T.W_EVRAKTYPE = "S4"
		STOK20T.W_EVRAKNO = SFDC32E.EVRAKNO
		STOK20T.W_TRNUM = SFDC32T.TRNUM(i)
	Next
	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
'	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"
	ret = Doc2.Save_Voucher
	SonOpIseUretimFisiKes = ret
	if ret <> 0 Then
		Doc1.Delete_Voucher
	End if
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function SonOpMu(MPSNo, JobNo)
	SonOpMu = False
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", JobNo, "JOBNO")
	if trim(SrNum) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("f23f23sdgh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		SonOpMu = True
	End if
End Function
Function UretimFisiniSil(EvrakNo, TrNum)
	SeriNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SFDC32T", "SERINO", EvrakNo & TrNum, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	QStr = "W_EVRAKTYPE='S4'&W_EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&W_TRNUM='" & TrNum & "'"
	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select1("sdfwegwhs", "STOK20T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S20T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		UretimFisiniSil = 0 
		Exit Function
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher S20T1.EVRAKNO(1)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then 
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Delete_Voucher
		Exit Function
	Else
		For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S20T.SERINO(i)) = trim(SeriNo) Then
				S20T.DeleRowNoUI i
			End if
		Next
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	End if
End Function
Sub EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SFDC32T.SERINO(i)), 30)
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("adf3efsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB.SF_MIKTAR <> SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if Not DocYaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
					Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
					DocYaratildi = True
				End if
				Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
				d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub CalcOpMiktar(MPSNo, IsNo, BuOpTamamlananMiktar, OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sd2g24sdfgdsg", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM<'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = -1
	Else
		BuOpYMamulMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_YMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO")
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = Doc.Div(M10T.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR(1) * BuOpYMamulMiktar, M10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(1))
	End if
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("dfw34gsg", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = 0
	if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = MTableT.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
	BuOpTamamlananMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO") + BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar + _
						STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
End Sub
Function HammaddeYok(IsNo, UretilecekMiktar)
	HammaddeYok = False
	MPSNo = Left(IsNo, 10)
	SiraNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_SIRANO", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	MPSYMamulMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_YMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO")

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("df24gsgs", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & SiraNo & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		Depo = Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "IM")
		IhtiyacMiktari = Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(i) *  UretilecekMiktar, MPSYMamulMiktar)
		if Depo < IhtiyacMiktari Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu operasyonda kullanýlan " & trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)) & " kodlu malzeme IM deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeYok = True
		End if
	Next
End Function                             

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SS13 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG7
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kalite Ölçüm Sonuçlarý Giriþ Ekraný (Silmeyin!!!!)
Kod : 
Dim Q10T
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT00')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT01')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "KOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "KOD_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "LOTNUMBER","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "LOTNUMBER_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "TEZGAH_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "TEZGAH_KODU_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "OPERASYON","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "OPERASYON_AV"

End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", MTable.MPSNO, "")
	Call KOD_AV()
End Sub
Sub KOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable.KOD, "")
End Sub
Sub TEZGAH_KODU_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.TEZGAH_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", MTable.TEZGAH_KODU, "")
End Sub
Sub OPERASYON_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.OPERASYON_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", MTable.OPERASYON, "")
End Sub
Sub LOTNUMBER_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set S31E = Doc.Select1("aefa3fae", "SFDC31E", "LOTNUMBER='" & MTable.LOTNUMBER & "'", "", -1)
	if S31E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MTable.KOD = S31E.STOK_CODE
		Call KOD_AV()

		MTable.TEZGAH_KODU = S31E.X_T_ISMERKEZI
		Call TEZGAH_KODU_AV()

		MTable.OPERASYON = S31E.OPERASYON
		Call OPERASYON_AV()
	End if
End Sub
Function GFLSMik_Round(Sayi)
	GFLS_Mik = Doc.Customp_Gets("GFLS_MIK")
	Nokta = InStr(1, GFLS_Mik, ".")
	Ondalik = Doc.CDbl(trim(Mid(GFLS_Mik, Nokta + 1)))
	GFLSMik_Round = Doc.Round(Sayi, Ondalik)
End Function
Sub KaliteTestSonuclariGirisi()
	Set MTable1=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_KALITE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	
	Set MTable1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	QVLCODE1 = Doc.Customp_Gets("QVLCODE1")
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "K") > 0 Then
		StokKodu = MTable1.KOD
	End if
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "L") > 0 Then
		LotNo = MTable1.LOTNUMBER
	End if
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "S") > 0 Then
		SeriNo = MTable1.SERINO
	End if

	Set par = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
	par.SetParameter "CREATE_QVAL_DEF_TABLE","E" 

	par.SetParameter "ICODE", MTable1.TEZGAH_KODU 
	par.SetParameter "OCODE", MTable1.OPERASYON 

	ret = Doc.QVAL01E_CREATEEDITTABLE("___QVAL___",StokKodu, LotNo, SeriNo, par)
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")

'	TblStr = 	par.GetParameter("_QVAL_DEF_TABLE")
'msgbox TblStr
	TblStr = "_QVAL_DEF_TABLE_110234"
	Set Q10T = Doc.GetTableObject(TblStr)

'	Call AlanAdlariniveEventleriEkle(Q10T, KaliteTable)

	MTable.KOD = MTable1.KOD
	MTable.LOTNUMBER = MTable1.LOTNUMBER
	MTable.SERINO = MTable1.SERINO
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "KALITE",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Sub KaliteYaz()
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")
	ret=Doc.SetSaveable(TRUE)
	KaliteTable.Exec "Save"
	ret=Doc.SetSaveable(FALSE)
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Kalite parametreleri kaydedildi", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(Q10T, KaliteTable)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
	En = 0
	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1024
		Boy = 690
	Else
		En = En - 25
		Boy = Boy - 60
	End if 
	TabEn = En - 5
	TabBoy = Boy - 80

	GridEn = En - 5
	GridBoy = TabBoy - 65

	FormStr = "FORM KALITE HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Kalite" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET FIXEDPITCH_EDIT=1" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "DEFINEFONT ID=""SABIT"" FACE=""Courier New"" POINT=10 //BOLD=Y " & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "DEFINEFONT ID=""BOLD"" FACE=""MS Sans Serif"" POINT=8 BOLD=Y " & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "GROUPBOX TEXT="""" AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=35 TOP=+0 WIDTH=300p HEIGHT=80p BORDER=BUMP" & vbCrLf
	
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+5p TOP=+10p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Stok Kodu'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.KOD" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+0 TOP=E+3p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Lot No'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.LOTNUMBER" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+0 TOP=E+3p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Seri No'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.SERINO" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGROUPBOX" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCX" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=80 TOP=15p WIDTH=15 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KaliteYaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+5p WIDTH=15 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbcrlf


	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCX" & vbCrLf

	QValStr = GetQValStr(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	FormStr = FormStr & QValStr & vbCrLf
	'FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetQValStr(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	Ust = 4
	IkinciBlok = 11
'	QValStr = "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
 	QValStr = "FRAME AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=1P TOP=E+5p WIDTH=1010p HEIGHT=500P HORIZONTALSCROLLBAR=NO BORDER=0" & vbcrlf
	For i = 1 to Q10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(Q10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = Q10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if

		Aciklama1 = Q10T.VARASPNAME(i)

		GrupAciklama = Doc.GetGecoValue("QVALSC", "AD", Q10T.VARCODE(i), -1)

		Aciklama = GrupAciklama
		AlanTuru = trim(Q10T.VARTYPE(i))
		AlanGenisligi = Q10T.VARLEN(i)
		Grupkodu = trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))

'		if i >= IkinciBlok Then Left1 = 68 Else Left1 = 1
'		if i = IkinciBlok Then QValStr = QValStr & "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
		Left1 = 1
		QValStr = QValStr & "RTEXT LEFT=" & Left1 & " TOP=E+0 T='" & Aciklama & "' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Else
			QValStr = QValStr & "EDITTEXT "
		End if
		QValStr = QValStr & "LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 "
		if AlanGenisligi > 50 Then QValStr = QValStr & "WIDTH=50"
		QValStr = QValStr & "D=" & KaliteTable.GetAppName & "." & AlanAdi & vbCrLf
		MinMax = ""
		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i)) <> "" Then
			MinMax = "Min.:" & trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i))
		End if
		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i)) <> "" Then
			if trim(MinMax) <> "" Then MinMax = MinMax & " - "
			MinMax = MinMax & "Max.:" & trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i))
		End if
		if trim(MinMax) <> "" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 T='" & MinMax & "' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
		End if

		if trim(GrupAciklama) <> trim(Aciklama1) Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+5p TOP=+0 T='(" & trim(Aciklama1) & ")' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
			Aciklama = Aciklama & " - " & trim(Aciklama1)
		End if


	Next
 	QValStr = QValStr & "ENDFRAME"
	GetQValStr = QValStr
End Function
Sub FIELD_AV()
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")
	AlanAdi = Doc.GetGUIControl_Field()
	Set Q10T1 = Doc.Select2("wef23fsdf", Q10T.GetAppName, "VERIFIKASYONTEXT4='"& AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if Q10T1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) = "" and trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) = "" Then Exit Sub
		
		AltLimit = -999999999999999999999999999999999999999999
		UstLimit = 9999999999999999999999999999999999999999999

		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" Then AltLimit = Doc.Cdbl(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1)
		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then UstLimit = Doc.Cdbl(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2)
	
		Deger = KaliteTable.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, -1)
		if Deger >= AltLimit and Deger <= UstLimit Then Exit Sub

		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" and trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & AltLimit & " ile " & UstLimit & " arasýnda olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Elseif trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & AltLimit & " den büyük veya eþit olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Elseif trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & UstLimit & " den küçük veya eþit olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		End if	
	End if
End Sub
Sub AlanAdlariniveEventleriEkle(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	For i = 1 to Q10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(Q10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = Q10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i) = AlanAdi

		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i)) <> "" or trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i)) <> "" Then
			KaliteTable.Setd7FieldEvent AlanAdi,"AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "FIELD_AV"
		End if
	Next
End Sub
